みずほ経済 EXPRESS

日本の原油輸入価格は今後さらに上昇へ 
～物価上振れリスクが強まれば、一段の利上げ加速も～

調査部 主任エコノミスト 東深澤 武史 
池田 亮平 
エコノミスト 阿部 大樹 
シニア日本経済エコノミスト 越山 祐資 
080-1069-4919  takeshi.higashifukasawa@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 日銀は物価上振れ抑制に舵。中東情勢に起因する物価押し上げリスクは予断を許さず 
9 月日銀会合後の会見で植田総裁は「政策局面が変化した」と述べた（図表 1） 。これまで日銀は、
基調的物価の押し上げを金融政策運営の重点としてきたが、足元では 2％前後の物価上昇が定着しつ
つあるとみられる。こうしたもとで、金融政策運営の重点は物価上振れの回避に移っており、そのた
めに緩和的な金融環境をなるべく早く脱することが重視される。日銀の基本的なスタンスが、連続利
上げや、0.25％の利上げ幅を上回る大幅利上げは回避するのが望ましいというものであることも踏ま
えると、今後の利上げペースは 3 カ月に一回程度、0.25％ずつが基本になるだろう 1。 
ただし、金融政策の重点が物価上振れ回避にシフトしているなかでは、よりフォワードルッキング
な政策運営が求められる 2。したがって、 原油調達コストの上昇や円安など、 物価上振れリスクの強ま
り次第で利上げペースが速まる可能性がある。先行きの物価を押し上げる主なリスク要因として、日
図表1 9月会合後の総裁会見のポイント 図表2 WTI原油先物

### Page 2

2

銀は、原油高等の中東情勢影響、AI 関連需要の強さ、円安の三点を挙げている。このうち、原油価格
は足元で再び上昇しており、先行きの不確実性も大きい。中東情勢は、物価を取り巻く現下最大のリ
スクだろう。 
■ 不安定な情勢下で再び原油価格が上昇。タンカー運賃も急騰しダブルパンチの様相 
WTI原油先物は、9月に入って一時1バレル=100ドル台と、5月中旬以来の水準に上昇した （図表2） 。
足元では原油価格は幾分低下しているものの、水準としては依然高い状態が続いている。背景には、
中東産原油の供給懸念が強まったことがある。7月下旬以降、 サウジアラビアとイエメン・フーシ派に
よる戦闘が開始し、 ホルムズ海峡を代替する重要な原油輸送ルートとして活用されてきたバブ ・ エル ・
マンデブ海峡の通航リスクが高まった。 また、9月に入り、 サウジアラビアの原油パイプラインが攻撃
を受けて停止したと伝わると、中東産原油の域外輸送が一段と困難になるとの見方が広がった。 
中東情勢が再度悪化するなかで上昇しているのは原油価格だけではない。原油価格の上昇を大幅に
上回る勢いで、原油タンカーの運賃が急騰している（図表3）。8月以降に上昇基調が鮮明となり、9月
中旬には一時、中東情勢悪化前（2026年初）の約46倍、情勢悪化後の3～7月頃と比べても約3.2倍に達
した。 海峡航行リスクが高まるもと、 積み替えや迂回によって原油輸送の効率が悪化しているためだ。
海事データを活用して商品市場を分析しているベルギーの調査会社Kplerによると、 ホルムズ海峡の内
側にあるペルシャ湾から原油を運び出す船と、海峡の外側から最終目的地まで運ぶ船を分け、洋上で
原油を積み替える方式が広がっているようだ。 長距離輸送を担う船が危険な航路を通らずに済む反面、
積み替え作業や待機に時間を要するため、船を次の輸送に回しにくくなる。また、危険な海域を避け
るために遠回りする輸送も増え、船が一度の航海に費やす日数も長くなっている。より多くの輸送資
源が必要となったことで船舶需給がひっ迫し、タンカー運賃が大きく押し上げられている格好だ。

図表3 原油タンカーの運賃 図表4 原油輸入単価と原油価格

■ 今後は日本の原油調達コストが上昇。企業間取引価格は反転加速を予想 
原油価格の上昇に原油タンカー運賃の急騰が重なり、先行きの日本の原油輸入単価（単位量あたり
の原油調達コスト）は一段と上昇する見込みだ3。 中東産原油が日本に輸送されるまでには1～2カ月程
度の期間を要するため、8月中旬以降の原油価格上昇 ・原油タンカー運賃上昇は10月以降の原油輸入単
価を押し上げ、その後国内の企業間取引価格へと影響が波及していくと考えられる。足元の原油価格
とタンカー運賃を踏まえると、10月の原油輸入単価は1バレル＝約140ドル程度と、中東情勢悪化後で
最も高い水準に上昇する可能性が高い（図表4）。この影響を輸入全体の平均価格（円建てベース）で
みると、10月の伸びは前年比＋29％程度と、中東情勢悪化後に最も輸入物価の伸びが加速した6・7月
に迫る勢いになる計算だ。さらに、その後は、石油化学製品などの関連財を中心に、国内の企業間取
引価格が上昇しよう。 足元で上昇圧力が一服しつつある国内企業物価は再び加速し、11月頃に同＋7％
台の伸びになると予想する（図表5）。 
加えて、原油輸入金額が膨らんで貿易収支が悪化すれば、円安圧力が強まる可能性がある。原油の
代替調達が進展した6月以降は1兆円に迫る貿易赤字が続いているが、特に10月以降は原油輸入単価が
上昇するもとで本邦貿易赤字が拡大する公算が大きく、実需面からの円安圧力が強まりやすい（図表
6） 。 足元の為替相場は、 米国の利上げ観測を背景に円安に振れやすい地合いだ。 そこに実需の動きが
重なり、1ドル＝160円を上回るようなドル高円安が進行すれば、輸入物価の上振れを通じて、国内企
業物価の伸びが一層高まるリスクがある。 
■ 物価上昇圧力が長期化する場合は利上げペース加速も視野に 
以上のように、当面の企業間取引価格に対しては押し上げ圧力が持続する公算が大きい。物価上昇
圧力が長期化するほど消費者物価への価格転嫁姿勢も強まる可能性があるだけに、日銀は物価上振れ
に対する高い警戒レベルを維持することになろう。 
仮に物価上振れリスクが大きく高まる場合には、 連続利上げ ・大幅利上げの可能性も排除できない。
中東情勢の混乱が長期化し、27年以降も原油輸入単価が一段と上振れることが見込まれる場合、ある

### Page 4

4

いは1ドル=160円を持続的に上回る水準まで円安が進展する場合には 、連続利上げや大幅利上げのシ
ナリオが現実味を帯びてくるだろう。

1 中立金利の水準は不透明であり、利上げの影響はタイムラグをともなって現れる。連続利上げ・大幅利上げによって意図せず
金融引き締めに転じれば、経済へのマイナス影響が必要以上に強まるリスクがある。 
2 中東情勢悪化以降で最初の利上げを決定した 6 月会合を振り返っても、日銀は川上の企業物価の加速を理由の一つとして利上
げに踏み切っている。6 月会合の声明文において日銀は、国内企業物価上昇率が前年比＋6.3％（5 月時点の速報ベース）に加速
したことを踏まえ、 「企業間取引におけるやや速いスピードでの価格転嫁が、消費者段階における幅広い品目の価格上昇に波及
していく可能性がある」と評価した。 
3 中東情勢悪化以降、日本の原油輸入単価は国際市況から大きく上振れている。背景として、主な代替調達先である米国産原油
が需給ひっ迫の懸念等から値上がりし、割高になったことなどが挙げられる。経済産業省発表によると、秋口以降は中東産原油
の調達が増加し、割高な米国産原油に対する依存度は低下すると見込まれるものの、本文記載のように中東産原油の輸送コスト
が大幅に高まっていることから、先行きも割高感の解消は見込みづらい。

Document 031: 20261019_投資週報_通膨升溫增添波動，台積財報展現韌性

金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 19 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國 9 月通膨升溫，Fed 年底前再升息機率仍高： 美國 9 月 CPI 年增 3.7%，高於 8 月的
3.4%；核心 CPI 年增率亦由 2.4%升至 2.6%，顯示通膨壓力不只來自能源，核心價格亦有回
升。隔日的 PPI 年增率則由 5.6%升至 6.3%，反映上游成本壓力仍強。通膨重新升溫，使Fed
即使面對就業降溫，也難以快速轉向寬鬆，市場對年底前再度升息的預期仍高。
◼ 台積電財報創新高，AI 需求強勁但市場轉向檢視融資風險：台積電 10/15 公布其第三季淨利

達 7,631 億元、年增 69%，連續第六季刷新紀錄，第三季美元營收 467.2 億美元，並預估第
四季營收將升至 523～543 億美元 ； 公司同時將2026 全年營收成長預估提高至約 44%， 反映
AI 晶片與先進製程需求仍極為強勁。然而，高利率環境使市場開始檢視 AI 資料中心業者的槓
桿與融資能力，CoreWeave 約 500 億美元債務引發信用疑慮，引發市場波動。
◼ 中東衝突再度推高能源風險 ， 油價高檔加劇全球通膨壓力 ：胡塞武裝上週攻擊沙烏地阿美設施 ，
伊朗亦表示採取新的海上攻擊策略，使荷莫茲海峽與波斯灣航運風險再度升高。10/16 布蘭特
原油與西德洲原油期貨雙雙收在近三週高點。
市場展望
◼ 科技股中期趨勢料將不變 ， 但短線波動勢將加劇 ：近期市場同時面臨通膨升溫 、AI 資本支出疑
慮與重要科技財報公佈在即等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能受到壓抑。在利率維持高檔及市
場評價偏高的環境下，市場短線料將隨財報結果與市場預期而出現較大波動。然而，上週台積
電與 ASML 財報結果整體優於預期 ， 可見AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡象 ，

科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，惟後續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出變動及科技子
產業的表現分化現象。
◼ 中天期美債與投資級債債券兼顧息收與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性 ， 加上政府與企業發債供
給維持高檔，可能使市場利率持續處於較高水準。雖然投資級債信用利差位於相對低檔，但企
業獲利、現金流及償債能力仍具支撐，加上較高的美國公債基準利率，使投資級債的利息收益
仍具吸引力。相較之下，短天期債券面臨未來利率下降後的再投資風險，長天期債券則較容易
受到公債供給增加 、 期限溢酬及利率波動影響 ， 因此中天期美國公債與投資債券較能兼顧息收
與利率風險。

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引

1

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國經濟維持溫和擴張，勞動市場降溫與通膨黏著牽動聯準會政策 
經濟 
⚫ 2026 年第 2 季美國實質 GDP 季增年率終值上修至 2.2%， 惟低於第1 季
之 2.5%。成長主要由個人消費及固定投資帶動，其中消費貢獻 2.51 個
百分點；惟進口大增 12.6%，致淨出口拖累 1.10 個百分點。另實質私人
國內最終銷售成長 4.6%，顯示內需仍具韌性。 
⚫ 9 月美國非農就業僅增 2.9 萬人，遠低於前月及近一年平均，失業率升
至 4.2%， 薪資增幅續降 ， 顯示企業聘僱動能轉弱 。 惟勞動市場尚未明顯
惡化，後續仍須關注就業降溫對聯準會利率決策之影響。 
⚫ 8 月美國職位空缺數由 733.5 萬個降至 707.9 萬個，職缺率降至 4.3%，
顯示企業徵才需求略為降溫；惟聘僱人數增至 519.2 萬人。主動離職及
裁員規模同步下降，反映勞工轉職意願與企業人力調整趨於審慎，勞動
市場供需大致維持平衡。 
⚫ 9 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數為 54.5，連續第九個月維持擴張。
新訂單、就業及未完成訂單同步改善，顯示需求仍具韌性；惟生產、出
口及進口動能略為放緩，且原物料成本持續攀升，加上關稅政策、地緣
政治及供應鏈不確定性仍存，製造業仍面臨部分壓力。 
⚫ 8 月美國 PCE 物價指數月增 0.3%、年增 3.4%，核心 PCE 月增 0.2%、
年增 3.0%， 顯示通膨大致持穩但短期物價壓力略升 。 另一方面 ， 個人消
費支出月增 0.9%、實質消費成長 0.6%，帶動儲蓄率降至 4.1%，顯示家
庭需求仍具韌性；後續須關注消費熱度及通膨對聯準會政策之影響。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 近期聯準會官員立場趨於分歧，理事 Michael Barr 認為能源價格及 AI
投資熱潮可能延緩通膨降溫 ， 未來仍可能進一步升息 ； 惟John Williams、
Philip Jefferson 及 Michelle Bowman 等官員則主張暫無急於升息的必要 ，
傾向觀察後續經濟數據。加以 9 月非農就業明顯放緩，市場對 10 月升
息預期大幅降溫，焦點轉向 12 月會議。 
⚫ 台積電傳評估與馬斯克旗下 Terafab 合作赴德州設廠，雙方仍處討論階
段。合作可能由台積電主導營運，或由 Terafab 持多數股權、台積電提
供技術 。 若成真 ， 將擴大台積電美國布局 ， 並支援Tesla、SpaceX 及 xAI
晶片需求。

一、 美國—美國經濟維持溫和擴張，勞動市場降溫與通膨黏著牽動聯準會政策 
2026 年第二季美國實質 GDP 季增年率終值由前次估計之 1.5%上修至 2.2%，
惟仍低於第一季之 2.5%， 顯示經濟持續擴張但成長動能略有放緩 。 此次上修主要
反映投資、消費及政府支出優於原先估計。就成長結構觀察，個人消費支出仍為
主要動能，貢獻 2.51 個百分點，其中服務及商品消費分別貢獻 1.57 及 0.94 個百
分點；私人投資貢獻 0.82 個百分點，以設備投資及智慧財產權投資表現強勁，惟
庫存調整拖累 0.53 個百分點。對外貿易方面，出口成長 5.0%，但進口大幅增加
12.6%， 致淨出口拖累GDP 成長 1.10 個百分點 。 另反映內需基本面的實質私人國
內最終銷售成長 4.6%， 高於第一季之1.8%， 顯示民間需求明顯增強 。 物價方面 ，
第 2 季 PCE 及核心 PCE 物價指數分別上升 5.0%及 3.3%， 顯示通膨壓力仍偏高 。
整體而言，美國經濟仍由消費與固定投資支撐，惟進口快速增加及通膨黏著性，
仍為後續經濟前景的重要觀察重點。 
2026 年 9 月美國非農就業人數增加 2.9 萬人 ， 明顯低於上月修正後的13.3 萬
人，也低於過去 12 個月平均每月增加 4.5 萬人的水準；失業率小幅升至 4.2%，
整體仍維持在 3 月以來 4.1%至 4.3%的區間，反映勞動市場整體變化有限，惟新
增就業力道已轉趨疲弱。家庭調查方面，勞動參與率升至 61.8%，就業人口比為
59.2%，均較上月小幅上升；長期失業人數約 194.4 萬人，占總失業人口 27.1%，
大致維持穩定。產業別方面，主要產業就業變化有限，其中醫療保健增加 1.7 萬
人、營建業增加 1.1 萬人、製造業增加 0.9 萬人，金融活動則減少 0.7 萬人，政府
部門減少 1.7 萬人。薪資方面，私人部門平均時薪升至 37.81 美元，月增 0.1%、
年增 3.0%，平均每週工時維持 34.4 小時。另 7 月與 8 月新增就業合計下修 6 萬
人，反映前二月就業動能較初估疲弱。整體而言，9 月美國新增就業明顯放緩、
失業率小幅上升且薪資增速降溫，企業聘僱動能較先前轉弱；惟失業率仍維持於
近期區間，勞動市場尚未出現明顯惡化跡象，後續仍須關注企業聘僱需求是否持
續降溫，以及對聯準會利率決策的影響。 
2026 年 8 月美國職位空缺數(Job Openings) 由上月修正後之 733.5 萬個減少
25.6 萬個至 707.9 萬個 ， 職缺率由4.4%降至 4.3%， 顯示企業用人需求略有降溫 ；

惟聘僱人數由 514.6 萬人小幅增加至 519.2 萬人，聘僱率由 3.2%升至 3.3%。總離
職人數由 512.8 萬人減少至 507.0 萬人，離職率維持於 3.2%。就離職結構觀察，
主動離職(Quits)人數由 308.9 萬人降至 306.6 萬人，主動離職率維持 1.9%；裁員
與解僱(Layoffs and Discharges) 人數則由 170.2 萬人降至 164.1 萬人，裁員率由
1.1%降至 1.0%。產業別方面，職位空缺增加主要來自零售業、金融保險業、資訊
業及休閒餐飲業 ； 聘僱人數則以政府部門及製造業增幅較為明顯 。 整體而言 ，8 月
美國職缺數較前月下滑，反映企業徵才需求略有降溫，但聘僱活動仍維持穩定，
且主動離職與裁員規模同步下降，顯示勞工轉職意願及企業人力調整趨於審慎，
勞動市場供需仍大致維持平衡。 
2026 年 9 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數(Manufacturing PMI) 為 54.5，
較上月之 54.6 微降 0.1 點，惟已連續第九個月維持於 50 以上擴張區間，顯示製
造業景氣持續擴張。細項觀察，新訂單指數由53.7 升至 55.3，需求動能續增；生
產指數由 58.3 降至 56.7，雖仍維持擴張，但成長速度略有放緩；就業指數由51.2
升至 52.7，反映企業聘僱需求持續增加。供應商交貨指數由 59.3 微降至 59.0，交
貨延遲情況仍存；庫存指數則由 50.6 降至 48.6，企業備貨態度轉趨保守。此外，
價格指數由 71.1 升至 77.9，原物料及投入成本壓力升高；未完成訂單指數由51.8
升至 56.4，訂單去化變慢。另一方面，新出口訂單指數由53.2 降至 50.9，進口指
數亦由 52.5 降至 51.0，外需成長動能及企業備料需求略為放緩。整體而言，9 月
美國製造業活動持續穩健擴張，新訂單、就業及未完成訂單同步改善，顯示製造
業景氣仍具韌性；惟出口及進口增速放緩、投入成本壓力升高，加上關稅政策、
地緣政治風險及供應鏈不確定性仍存，後續發展仍值得持續關注。 
2026 年 8 月美國個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數月增率由上月之 0.1%升至 0.3%，
年增率維持於 3.4%； 扣除食品及能源後之核心PCE 月增率由 0.1%升至 0.2%，年
增率亦持平於 3.0%，顯示整體通膨大致持穩，惟短期物價漲勢較前月略有回升。
另一方面，個人所得月增 0.2%，可支配所得(DPI)月增 0.3%，實質可支配所得持
平；個人消費支出月增 0.9%，實質個人消費支出成長 0.6%，顯示民間消費表現
明顯回溫。由於消費增幅高於所得增幅，個人儲蓄率由前月之 4.6%降至 4.1%。
整體而言，8 月美國通膨表現大致持穩，但消費支出顯著回升，反映家庭需求仍
具韌性；後續仍須持續關注消費熱度、通膨走勢及其對聯準會貨幣政策路徑之影
響。

近期聯準會官員談話呈現鷹鴿分歧。聯準會理事 Michael Barr 認為，中東局
勢推升能源價格，以及 AI 相關投資熱潮帶動需求增長，均可能阻礙通膨回落至
2%目標 ， 未來仍不排除進一步升息的必要性 ； 惟 紐約聯準銀行總裁 John Williams、
聯準會副主席 Philip Jefferson 及金融監理副主席 Michelle Bowman 則強調，在 9
月升息後，當前並無急迫性立即進一步收緊政策，應先觀察後續通膨、就業及金
融市場數據變化，再評估是否有進一步調整利率的必要。受官員談話轉趨審慎，
以及 9 月非農就業表現疲弱影響，市場對 10 月 FOMC 會議再度升息的預期明顯
降溫，政策焦點逐漸轉向 12 月會議及年底前是否仍有一次升息空間。 
晶圓代工龍頭台積電傳出正評估與馬斯克旗下 Terafab 合作，在美國德州布
局晶圓廠，馬斯克也於社群平台 X 證實雙方正在討論，並表示 「目前僅在討論階
段 ， 但可能會有進展」 。 目前浮上檯面的合作模式包括由台積電持有並營運新廠 ，
SpaceX 或 Terafab 透過入股及承諾採購晶圓參與；另一方案則由 Terafab 掌握多
數股權 ， 台積電提供製程技術與晶圓廠營運經驗 。Terafab 旨在因應 Tesla、SpaceX
及 xAI 快速成長的 AI 晶片需求，首期投資規模達 168 億美元。若合作成真，不
僅可望成為台積電繼亞利桑那州後在美國的新布局，也凸顯先進製程與穩定晶片
供應的重要性，但目前具體合作方式、時程與投資內容仍未正式定案。 
二、歐元區—法國殖利率大幅上升，不利於投資及消費表現 
 歐元區 9 月 HICP 通膨 、 核心通膨(不含食品及能源)分別上升 0.6、0.1 個百分
點至 3.8%、2.5%， 主因國際原油價格上漲 ， 使能源通膨上升4.5 個百分點至 18.8%，
並透過肥料、物流成本上漲，間接推升食品通膨 0.3 個百分點至 1.4%。另外，服
務通膨上升 0.2 個百分點至 3.2%，近三個月服務通膨大致呈現橫向波動，部分反
映旅遊旺季因素影響。基於布蘭特原油持續位於每桶 90 美元以上，預期年底前
仍將對通膨帶來額外壓力，後續須關注能源漲價可能會透過運輸、物流等成本管
道傳導至核心商品及服務價格，進而提高核心通膨黏性。 
 其他經濟數據包含 ：(1)歐元區 8 月失業率持平 6.4%， 失業人數增加2.6 萬人 ，
主要是義大利勞動參與人數增加所致，不過 ，基於PMI 顯示近期德國勞動雇用需
求持續增溫，將有助於穩定勞動市場表現；(2)歐元區第二季家庭儲蓄率下降 0.2
個百分點至 14.2%，顯示通膨壓力削弱民眾購買力，致儲蓄暫時性減少；(3)歐元
區第二季房價年增率為 4%， 較第一季降0.6 個百分點 ， 主因法國中古屋面臨嚴格

環保法規，須負擔高昂翻修成本，使法國房價呈現連三季下滑所拖累。 
 近一週法國 10 年期公債殖利率升至 4.9%以上，與德國公債殖利率差距擴大
至 125 個基點以上，主要反映市場對法國主權公債可持續性疑慮升溫。儘管目前
政府計畫將 2027 年財政赤字縮限至 5%，惟政府在國會並無穩定多數席次，即使
能以行政命令強行通過預算 ， 也可能面臨倒閣風險 ；此外 ，市場對於2027 年 4 月
總統大選後，新總統能否取得國會足夠支持，推動削減赤字與控制債務的改革亦
缺乏信心。因此未來法國仍可能面臨殖利率進一步上升與財政壓力加劇的風險，
暫時不利於投資及消費信心。 
三、日本—景氣溫和復甦，但消費放緩與通膨升溫風險升高 
 8 月工業生產月減 1.7%，主要係受到該月地震及豪雨等天然災害影響，導致
部分工廠暫時停產。從細項來看，汽車業受停工影響最為明顯，產量下滑6.8%，
電子產品產量亦減少 1.9%。 惟生產機械產量連續三個月成長 ， 加上核心資本財出
貨持續增加，顯示企業投資需求仍相當強勁，有助於支撐後續工業生產回升。從
企業預期來看，製造商預期9 月產量將成長 3.2%，顯示生產活動可望迅速回溫，
在供應鏈逐步恢復、庫存回補需求持續，以及穩健的企業資本支出支撐下，預計
第三季工業生產仍可維持正成長，且成長動能可望延續至年底。 
 8 月零售月減 1.2%，雖然表現略優於市場預期，但仍顯示民間消費動能正在
放緩。此次疲弱主要原因來自耐久財銷售下滑，其中家電銷售因空調等產品在換
機需求告一段落後明顯回落，汽車銷售也隨政府刺激措施效應逐漸消退而連續第
四個月下降。相較之下，食品、服裝等非耐久財銷售雖同步走弱，但降幅相對有
限 ， 反映薪資成長仍對消費提供部分支撐 。惟不利天候與通膨壓力仍是主要風險 ，
8 月至 9 月持續豪雨已影響服裝等非必需消費，而除米價以外的食品及商品價格
持續上漲，也正侵蝕家庭實質購買力，恐限制消費者信心與消費復甦力道。 
 9 月東京核心通膨年增率升至 2.7%，雖然東京都免費托兒及免費水費政策造
成的基期效應貢獻約 0.5 個百分點，但通膨升溫主因仍是價格上漲已由能源與食
品擴散至整體經濟。受石油價格上揚帶動，企業開始加速將原物料及薪資成本轉
嫁給消費者，帶動商品與服務價格普遍走高；租金通膨更升至 2%，創 1994 年以
來新高，公共交通及醫療等項目也開始調漲。隨著東京的物價壓力逐步反映至全
國數據，日本央行進一步升息的可能性升高。

用相關內容。

8

四、中國—為刺激內需、引導產業走向，人行漸近式放寬政策工具 
9 月官方製造業 PMI 上升 0.3 點至 50.1，為近三個月高點，新訂單、出口訂
單指數分別為 50.5、50，並帶動生產指數上升至 51.7，不過，庫存指數下降、就
業指數加速衰退、企業展望減弱，皆拖累 PMI 指數水準。此外，投入、產出物價
指數分別上升至 60.8、54，顯示成本壓力加劇，但售價調整速度依然較為遲緩，
不利於獲利表現。官方非製造業 PMI 上升 1.2 點至 50.2，其中建築業活動指數大
增 3.4 點至 50.3，脫離連續八個月的衰退區間，主要是政府加大財政刺激力度，
推動重大建設開工；非製造業的從業人員指數為 45.9，顯示就業情況仍顯嚴峻，
亦較不利於內需表現。 
鑒於內需動能疲弱，人行於 9 月 29 日宣布下調抵押補充貸款(該貸款本質是
將資金引導至政府希望發展的領域)利率自 1.75%降至 1.50%，並擴大適用範圍，
將水網、新型電網、算力網、新一代通信網、城市地下管網及物流網等建設納入
支持，有助於提振內需動能。未來若內需復甦力道不如預期，不排除人行將進一
步降準或擴大結構性貨幣工具支持經濟；至於全面降息則仍須兼顧人民幣匯率及
中美利差等因素，可能性相對較低。 
五、印度及東南亞—泰國、菲律賓出口均達 2 成以上的高速成長 
(1)新加坡：受服務業、零售暨其他商品業，以及食品價格漲幅擴大影響，8 月消
費者物價指數(CPI)年增率升至 2.3%、不包括住宿及個人交通費用之核心 CPI 年
增率升至 2.2%， 連續3 個月上升並創 2 年新高 。 受全球能源價格居高不下及氣候
條件不佳影響，預期新加坡進口食品、商品及服務成本將持續上升，惟國內生產
率成長及政府補貼政策將抑制部分服務業通膨，新加坡金管局(MAS)或在 10 月
「略微」緊縮貨幣政策。(2)泰國：8 月出口額年增 24.3%(346 億美元)，主要由工
業產品出口成長 27.1%所帶動，其中電腦及相關設備（年增 42.1%）、電話及零
件（151.2%） 及電路板 （19.2%） 增幅最為顯著，對美國出口年增48.7%、對中國
和日本分別成長 15.5%、19.6%。8 月進口額年增 25.1%(371 億美元)，以燃料 （年
增 42.8%） 、 資本貨物 （33.9%） 、 其他貨物 （27.4%） 、 原材料和中間產品 （21.3%）、
運輸設備（13.6%）為主。進出口相抵，8 月貿易逆差 24.8 億美元，較 7 月略收
窄，為連續第 11 個月出現逆差。(3)菲律賓：8 月出口額大增 27.8%(91 億美元)，
創兩年新高，主要得益於電子產品（年增 59.9%） ，尤其是半導體（73.5%）及黃

鴻海 (2317 TT) 
上修獲利效益預期

永豐觀點 
垂直整合能力，維持 AI 伺服器領先地位，營收規模擴大，上修獲利效益預期。 
 投資評價與建議 
研究部維持鴻海買進建議：鴻海集團於科技日展現垂直整合、精密加工能力，並憑
藉著規模化交付能力， 預期於伺服器市場維持四成以上市占率 、並能優先取得新世
代產品參考設計， 營收規模放大的同時體現獲利率效益， 獲利金額擴大以及營運資
金運用，並樹立組裝產業進入門檻。研究部參考近期公告之財務及產業資訊，預期
鴻海 2H26~2027 年營運具上修空間 ， 調整後預估鴻海 2026 年/2027 年稅後 EPS 
18.79 元/25.46 元。惟近期總體利率環境影響股價評價，目標本益比從 15 倍調整
至 14 倍，維持鴻海買進建議，目標價 356 元(14x2027FEPS)。

鴻海為專業 ODM/EMS 廠商，產品涵蓋手機、伺服器、平板、網通設備等終端產
品，以及各類電子零組件如連接器、連接線等，旗下重要子公司包含工業富聯
(601138 SH)、富智康(2038 HK)及鴻騰精密(6088 HK)，近年公司開始積極投入研
發資源在電動車 、 數位醫療 、 機器人 、AI、 半導體 、5G 等領域 。 主要客戶為Apple、
HP、Dell、Sony、Cisco、北美四大 CSP 等。2Q26 產品營收占比：消費暨智能
29%、雲端網路 51%、電腦終端 15%、元件及其他 5%。 
鴻海科技日展現垂直整合能力  統合零組件到機櫃生產：(1) 鴻海科技日強調展現
垂直整合能力及精密加工能力，提供客戶 one stop shop，垂直整合、設計製造服
務。鴻海具備伺服器 Level 1 到 Level 12 的生產能力，從螺絲、零組件、機構件
等至伺服器系統整機櫃。鴻海團隊從產品設計開始即整合組裝、良率、服務零組件
到整系統最佳化的解決方案。(2) 鴻海協助輝達、Google 打造 AI 工廠、AI 基礎
建設，以自動化縮短資料中心建造時程。(3) 施耐德電機、東元提供電力設備，與
鴻海進一步合作打造模組化資料中心。 4. 本田與三菱電機展示與鴻海在電動車未
來自動駕駛、駕駛監控系統、 ECU 等合作方向。 (5) 鴻海同時推出新電動車平
台,Model A+ 、K 系列。A+為休旅車，其特色為取消 B 柱讓車內寬敞，並將配置 
L4 自動駕駛感測器。Model K 為輕型卡車，提供短程 120 公里、長程 250 公里車
型選擇。 
展場上鴻海集團展出 AI 伺服器相關產品，1. 與 Vertiv 合作液冷基建的 AI Data 
Hall 解決方案。(2) 展出 NVIDIA DSX POD 解決方案，以及 Vera Rubin 世代 
Compute Tray、Switch Tray、CPO Switch、CPU Tray、LPU Tray 等相關產品。
(3) Google 下一代 TPU 將使用的 OCS 全光交換機。(4) 在 NVIDIA CPO Switch
使用 的精 密 光學 對位 機 構 件、FAU 光纖 連 接產 品。(5) 集團 公司 鴻 騰精密
(6088.HK)， 展出液冷相關零組件QD、Manifold； 電力相關零組件Busbar、Power 
Whip；主板上連接器 Paladin、SOCAMM 等。甚至下一代 Vera Rubin Ultra 將
採用 ZQD、POGO 連接器也已取得 NPI。 
美系大廠資本支出 2027 年上修至 6 成以上增長 建置需求未歇：上週美股法說會
後，統計 Bloomberg 美系五大 CSP 加上 Space X，2026 年資本支出從年增率從
分別從 102%上修至 103%，2027 年從 44%上修至 60%，2026 年及 2027 年資
本支出金額分別達 8,793 億美元及 1.41 兆美元，顯示AI 算力建置需求仍未停歇。 
上修 3Q26、4Q26 營收獲利預估：鴻海先前公告 3Q26 營收達 3.0 兆優於預期，
以及子公司工業富聯公告 3Q26 財報優於預期。以此推算鴻海營收擴大規模效益
帶來獲利表現上修，研究部預期營益率增長至 3.8%，稅後 EPS 上修至 5.10 元。
上修 4Q26 營收預期，主因3Q26 營運基期墊高，以及iPhone 維持出貨動能、GB
機櫃出貨量出貨量、Vera Rubin 機櫃單價跳升等，推算帶動營收季增 14%，稅後
EPS 上修至 5.85 元。

台光電(2383)     買進 報告日期：20261104 
                                                  研究員：陳彥翰

公司基本資料 
前日收盤價(元) 4,395.00  
目前股本(億元) 35.83

外資持股比例(%) 42.40  
投信持股比例(%) 11.88

融資餘額(張) 3,308

個股簡評 
高階銅箔基板產能供不應求，載板可望通過
驗證並成為新成長動能，以上將帶動產品組
合持續大幅優化，推升報價和毛利率，讓今
明兩年營運獲利續創歷史新高 。合庫預估  
2026 / 2027 年獲利分別成長 205% / 121%，
稅後每股盈餘  127.17 / 280.45  元，建議買
進，目標價 5,610 元 (2027 PER 20x)。 
業務營收比重

公司簡介  
台光電子材料股份有限公司成立於 1992 年，
初期主要供應  FR-4 銅箔基板與膠片；並於  
2013 年 ， 成為全球最大無鹵素基板供應商 ， 迄
今仍在市場保持領先地位 。 台光電持續開發出
各種優良的無鹵素新產品，從  Mid. Loss 、 
Low Loss、Very Low Loss、Ultra Low Loss 到 
Extreme Low Loss 等不同等級材料 ， 並符合各
種 高 精 密 先 進 PCB 技 術 ， 如Anylayer 、
mSAP、封裝載板、超高層板、高速傳輸及高
頻產品等各應用場域之需求。

2

營運展望 
1. 3Q26 需求強勁及漲價效應帶動營收獲利續創歷史新高：高速基材需求強勁、
帶動產品組合持續優化、及全品項漲價效應顯現，第三季營收續創歷史新高，
亦同時帶動毛利率進一步上揚。台光電第三季營收 609 億元、+28.8% QoQ、
+142.1% YoY， 毛利率 37.7%。 營業利益 187 億元、+46.8% QoQ、+274.5% YoY，
營業利益率 30.7%。歸屬母公司稅後淨利 145 億元，每股盈餘 40.47 元。期
末存貨總額提升至 271 億元，細項中原物料達 178 億元 、 製成品約 70 億元 、
在製品約 22 億元及商品約 7,774 萬元 ； 增備存貨主要著眼於高階原料市場吃
緊，藉由提前拉高原料水位以保障後續生產。 
2. 4Q26 需求仍然強勁將延續前幾季成長動能 ：第四季受惠產品結構與定價優勢 ，
將延續全產全銷強勁營運態勢，預估整體營收可望季增兩成。毛利率預期也因
定價優勢可進一步明顯提升。整體成長動能涵蓋 AI 伺服器、通用伺服器、高
階交換器、低軌衛星及記憶體模組等多重領域。高階基材整體價格持續調漲，
全產品線綜合漲價幅度預期落在 5 ~ 20% 之間 。基礎設施延續漲價效應及產品
組合優化將季增兩成，行動裝置因需求呈現持平，車用與工業則因產能優先挪
往高毛利產品，營收受排擠將季減約一成。 
3. 載板近期可望通過驗證並成為新一波成長動能：硬質銅箔基板涵蓋高密度互連
材料 (High Density Interconnect, HDI)、高速材料 (High Speed Digital, HSD) 與
載板 (Substrate) 三大範疇，雖然日韓對手高階 HDI 技術與台光電相仿，但擴
產停滯且侷限本土生產；台光電憑藉靈活量產規模與成本優勢，競爭力顯著勝
出。為追求極速傳輸與超低延遲，AI 系統架構加速走向模組化設計。單板晶片
密度倍增直接帶來線寬與線距極限微縮、高層數壓合等多重嚴苛的高階製程考
驗。台光電整合 HDI、高速與載板技術，推動「HDI 載板化」 ，確保板材歷經
多次高溫壓合仍具備載板級穩定性與可靠度。 
4. 產品組合持續往高階低損耗優化，新產能長約綁定將全產全銷：高階超低損耗
材料滲透率維持上升格局，M7+ 營收占比已從去年 50 ~ 55% 提升至 65 ~ 70%，
隨著 M9 下半年步入量產 ， 明年出貨規模放大 ， 佔比將進一步墊高至 70 ~ 75%。
產能也將自目前 585 萬張擴充至 2030 年 1,525 萬張。面對高階銅箔基板供
需日益緊俏且合規供應商有限下，終端客戶近期主動要求簽署長期供貨協議。 
5. 產品組合優化報價毛利率攀升，獲利將再創歷史新高：高階銅箔基板產能供不
應求，帶動產品組合持續大幅優化，推升報價和毛利率，今明年營運獲利將續
創歷史新高。 合庫預估 2026 年營收 2,086 億元、+121.3% YoY， 毛利率 35.3%。
營業利益 591 億元、+209.4% YoY，營業利益率 28.3%。歸屬母公司稅後淨利 
457 億元，每股盈餘 127.17 元。 
6. ESG (台光電在 2026 年上市公司治理評鑑結果為 6 ~ 20%)：台光電子針對各

項製造、服務與活動所衍生之空氣污染問題能加以管制及掌控。除了基本遵行

針對廠區環境保護投入相當金額，執行污染防治以維護環境品質。因應國際減
碳趨勢，以天然氣作為高碳過渡到低碳之能源。

### Page 2

months of the year), (2) new Kunshan capacity (total 1.2mn sheets/month or 21% of the
company’s current capacity) ramp up from December, and (3) the mass volume
shipment for several new AI projects (Vera Rubin + TPU v8 etc.) with a better demand
volume on the most high end M8.5/M9 CCL, we now expect EMC’s revenue to go up by
20% QoQ, and expect the GM will further expand to 40.9% (first time surpassing
40%).
For TUC, with an even faster product mix improvement speed (due to the lower base vs.

EMC) and the solid pricing hike uptrend for both high end and mid end CCL (15-20%
QoQ average pricing hike in 4Q), we expect the company to deliver a revenue growth
rate at 18% QoQ, and believe the GM will reach 40.8% (+3.9ppt QoQ) in 4Q26,
despite the slowing demand from key AI ASIC customer due to the transition period of
the ASIC projects whose capacity will be replaced by other high-end Ethernet switch
projects (also M8.5/M9 grade CCL).
Key update - EMC now plan to expand capacity to 15.25mn sheets/month from

5.85mn sheets/month capacity today, with solid demand
EMC’s BOD has approved additional capacity expansion plans extending into
2029/30E. EMC now targets year-end capacity of 8.70/10.45/11.95/15.25mn
sheets/month in 2027/28/29/30E (implying 27% 2026-30E CAGR), which is the first
time EMC announced its new capacity expansion plan after 1H28. EMC mentioned that
capacity expansion is supported by LTAs with customers who secure volumes (EMC does
not proactively initiate LTA negotiations). EMC indicated that despite its proactive

capacity expansion planned for 2027-30E, it would still be insufficient to meet with
customer demand secured under signed LTAs (demand under LTA extends at least 3Y
ahead).
EMC’s near-term capacity expansion remains intact, as the capacity expansion in
Kunshan (with additional 1.2mn sheets/month, 21% of current capacity) could
come online as early as Dec 2026 and continue through May 2027 (with new capacity
coming online every month), the expansion was originally scheduled in 2 phases, with

600k sheets/month each coming online in 1Q27 and 3Q27. The accelerated ramp-up
will offset the rescheduling of Guanyin capacity expansion, where the 300k sheet/month
expansions originally scheduled for 4Q26 have been deferred to 2H27, mainly due to
shortages of construction capacity in Taiwan as well as long equipment lead time +
Taiwan government’s strict policy on labor safety related requirement (however, we only
see delay in Taiwan, while all other plans are basically in-line or earlier than the

company’s original expectation).
Investment view - Solid margin beat + expanding margin, suggesting a stock
performance outlook; buy in the dip for both EMC & TUC
We notice that the BBG consensus for 2027 EMC/TUC GM is only at 38%/34%, and we
see upside risk to consensus EPS estimates for both EMC/TUC as the GM in 4Q could
both surpass 40-42%, without any possibility to see the GM to decrease in coming
quarters as the high-end CCL supply demand gap to further expand in coming years (see

here). As a result, we believe consensus 2027 GM could lift from 34-38% to 40-42%+ for
both companies in coming days, suggesting 10-25% EPS upward revision opportunity,
and recommend taking advantage to buy in this valuation dip (EMC/TUC only trades at
15x/12x of our bullish 2027 EPS estimate, which was far below the past 3 year range of
28 October 2026   2
Goldman Sachs Taiwan CCLFor the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM

### Page 3

20-30x).  
Moreover, EMC has mentioned all of its new capacity in coming years (capacity will 
expand by 2.6x in 2030 vs. 2026; see more details above) is still not large enough to 
support customers’ LTAs volume demand, suggesting an even more favorable high-end 
CCL pricing outlook in coming years (all the way to 2030), which is in-line with what we 
discussed in our TPCA takeaway report. We expect EMC/TUC will rise the like for like 
base pricing for M7+ CCL by 10-40%, and we now expect the similar pricing hike ratio in 
2027/28, with more room to adjust if the supply shortage issue continues.  
For the new CCL generation, we exect EMC to start shipment on M10 grade CCL from 
2H27, which is only for single customers’ AI GPU projects, but still implies a CCL 
migration trend in-line with our bullish view. For M9, we only see HGX R200 UBB and 
VR200 LPU adopt this solution in 2026, but see multiple new projects in 2027 will adopt 
this high ASP project (including several 1.6T ethernet switch projects, and Trainium 4/ 
TPU v9 could also see at least 50% adoption rate of the M9 CCL; moreover, the VR300 
rack should have 50%+ of its CCL content related to M9 grade CCL). We believe this new 
technology migration trend going into 2027 will further drive both companies’ ASP and 
profit in coming quarters, suggesting an even more favorable outlook for EMC/TUC’s EPS 
growth in coming years.  
Overall, we maintain our bullish view on high-end CCL players’ business outlook and 
margin expansion trend, and expect investors to start focusing on this solid margin 
expansion trend after this clean beat on GM/OPM in 3Q26; maintain Buy on 
EMC/TUC, with a new TP of NT$11,700/NT$3,460 (from NT$10,200/NT$3,010).

3-5+ years. We are positive on its market share trajectory in the server industry and 
expect EMC to increase its share from <10% in the Purley generation to 15-20%/20+% 
in the Whitley/Eagle Stream platforms. EMC is also proactively expanding its share in the 
switch market (40%/30%+ in 2024/23 vs. <5% before 2019), with an expanding AI 
customer base (only one 400G customer in 3Q20, to being one of the largest suppliers in 
2023), which should continue to benefit the company’s top/bottom line growth over 
time. We believe EMC’s leading position in the AI server CCL market should drive strong 
revenue growth and GM/OPM expansion in the long term, given that it is the major 
supplier for all Nvidia/Google/AWS AI server projects, and we expect it to maintain its 
leadership position. Considering its leading position in the high-end CCL industry, solid 
production skills and earnings growth outlook, we are Buy rated on the stock. With the 
shares trading at a 2026E P/E below AI server component suppliers and supported by a 
solid growth outlook, we view valuation as attractive. 
Key downside risks include: (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) 
rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and 
(3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Elite Material  
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$11,700 is based on 27x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(in line with the peak P/E multiple for AI component suppliers in the past 3 years).  
Key downside risks:  (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) rising 
trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and (3) 
rising competition from mainland China peers. 
Investment Thesis - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
TUC is a key high-end global CCL supplier that focuses on M7+ grade high-speed CCL 
(key applications include high-end switches (100G+) and AI server materials), with 20%+ 
market share in the past two years. The company continues to expect its unit market 
share in the server industry to increase from 15% in Whitley and Purley processors to 
higher in the Eagle Stream generation, and plans to launch two types of products for 
low-end and high-end customers (T2A and T2C) to gain market share. Also, strong 
growth in 400G/800G switch shipments and new 800G switch shipments, scheduled for 
launch in 2025-27, should continue to be a key demand driver for TUC, in our view. 
Additionally, the company is working on high-end AI projects with CSP and enterprise 
players, which we believe should drive revenue momentum in the long term. We view the 
stock as undervalued vs. Taiwan CCL peers on a P/E basis and rate it Buy.  
Key downside risks include: (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL 
segment; (2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch 
shipments globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$3,460 is based on a 22x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(+2x STDV higher than the past 3-year industry average P/E multiple).

Update
Hon Precision  | Asia Pacific
CPO  handler on track for 1Q27
mass production; stay OW
Key takeaways from yesterday's call:
• CPO/CPC: Has shipped  a small volume of  Insertion 4 OE CPO FT
qualification handlers in 4Q26, which are on track for mass production in
1Q27. Management expects qualification to start in November and end before
the year-end. It's also working on a CPC handler project with Japanese ATE
and a US customer who showed strong interest.

• Mega Tray Handler: Around 20-30% of customer bookings are Mega Trays,
while full system ASP would increase by at least 30% from original handlers.
Has also shipped the handler for customer qualification.
• Preliminary 2027 outlook:  To see strong revenue growth  in 2H27 from
Mega Trays and CPO-related demand as these higher ASP solutions will start
mass production in 1H27, with revenue recognition typically taking 3-6
months.
• Capacity outlook: 2027 remains on a 50% Y/Y pace, while the equipment

unit growth might be lower than 50% given the higher complexity of new
equipment.
Reiterate OW: Hon Precision is a key beneficiary of the expanding AI testing TAM.
We see the recent pullback as an attractive entry point, with the stock now trading
at 24x 2027e EPS vs. a 2025–28e earnings CAGR of >70%.

Hon Precision (7769.TW)
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture
the long-term value. We assume a 9.8% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium
6.0%), a payout ratio of 65%, a medium-term growth rate of 15%, and a terminal growth rate
of 4.0%. Our price target implies 77x 2026e, 45x 2027e and 27x 2028e P/Es.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Lite-On Tech

Upgrade to Buy; 3Q26 beat, better margin; 
solid AI outlook; lift PO to NT$288

Rating Change: BUY | PO: 288.00 TWD | Price: 240.50 TWD

Upgrade to Buy and raise PO to NT$288  
We upgrade Lite-On Tech (LoT) to Buy from Neutral with a higher PO of NT$288 (18x 
2027E EPS; prior NT$285 with valuation unchanged). We turn more positive on LoT 
after two straight quarters of results beat, especially with margin surprise. We lift 2026-
28E EPS by 1-8% (Exhibit 1), with still solid AI power-supply demand/order. Our 2026-
28E EPS is 4-7% above consensus (Exhibit 2). We expect the market to react more 
positively to LoT, with 3Q26 results beat, vs. Delta with 3Q26 results miss (see Robert 
Cheng’s Delta 3Q26 note), amid similar positive comments on long-term AI market 
demand. 
3Q26 beat: better margins, despite lighter sales 
LoT’s 3Q26 GM/OPM of 25.9%/15.1% came in better than BofAe of 25.3%/13.5% and 
consensus of 25.1%/13.6%, despite lighter sales (3%/5% below BofAe/consensus). We 
view the QoQ GM/OPM decline from record high in 2Q26 as largely expected as mgmt. 
had indicated at 2Q26 meeting that 1H26 GM of 24.7% represented a more sustainable 
level. Besides AI, better margins were also helped by improving product mix in IT/CE (i.e. 
higher mix from high-end ICT and LEO satellite) and higher automation level.  
4Q26 sales up QoQ; 2H26 GM above 1H; rising AI mix 
Similar to Delta, LoT also sees short-term shipment uncertainties/push-outs due to 
component shortage and power/electricity shortage for AI datacenter infrastructure. 
Nevertheless, the CEO remains long-term positive on AI demand. He also expects easing 
component/power constraints from 4Q26. Yet, mgmt. expects 4Q26 cloud sales to grow 
QoQ/YoY, led by ASIC/GPU/AMD projects, and 2H26 GM to be above 1H26 level (BofAe: 
26.3% in 4Q26, vs. 25.9%/24.7% in 3Q26/1H26). The CEO indicated 800VDC power rack 
mass shipment in 1Q27, after sample shipment in 4Q26. Mgmt. expects AI sales mix at 
30%+ in 2026 and 40-50% in 2027. This time, LoT raised 2026 capex to NT$19.7bn 
(prior NT$18.0bn) and sees higher level in 2027 (BofAe: NT$20.0bn).

Key Metrics

Company Sector 
Electronics Manufacturing Services

Company Description 
Lite-On Tech is a leading PC and server power-supply maker 
globally. Besides PC/server power-supply, its key products 
include optoelectronics (photo-coupler, LED lighting, 
outdoor lighting, auto lighting) and power-supply for game 
console and industrial. Key applications include AIoT, 5G, 
and smart life. Its key customers include HP, Dell, Lenovo, 
Microsoft, Sony, Nintendo, and global cloud hyperscalers. 
Lite-On Tech was founded in 1975 and headquartered in 
Taiwan.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Lite-On Tech in view of (1) its solid 
position in AI server power-supply as the second largest 
component maker globally and (2) expanding margins with 
improving product mix, rising scale, and better operating 
efficiency. We see consistently high cash dividend payout 
(over 50% since 2008) and solid balance sheet as share 
price downside supports.

Upgrade to Buy; raise PO to NT$288  
We upgrade Lite-On Tech (LoT) to Buy, from Neutral, with a higher PO of NT$288 (18x 
2027E EPS) from NT$285 with valuation unchanged. We turn more positive on LoT after 
two straight quarters of results beat, especially with margin surprise. We lift 2026-28E 
EPS by 1-8% (Exhibit 1) to reflect 3Q26 results beat, the latest guidance, and solid AI 
power-supply demand/order. Our 2026-28E EPS is 4-7% above consensus (Exhibit 2).  
LoT’s share price corrected 21% and 14% in the recent three months and one month, 
respectively, vs. key peer Delta’s (2308 TT; covered by Robert Cheng) -27%/-29% and 
TAIEX’s -2%/-6%. We expect more positive market reaction to LoT’s 3Q26 results beat, 
vs. Delta’s 3Q26 results miss (see Robert Cheng’s Delta 3Q26 note), amid similar 
positive comments on long-term AI market demand. LoT also has a cheaper valuation vs. 
Delta. LoT currently trades at 15x 2027E P/E, below Delta’s 24x and overall peers’ 
average of 20x (Exhibit 6).

3Q26 beat: better margins, despite lighter sales 
LoT posted 3Q26 results and held the physical analyst meeting on 30 Oct after market 
close. 3Q26 GM/OPM of 25.9%/15.1% came in better than BofAe of 25.3%/13.5% and 
consensus of 25.1%/13.6%, despite lighter sales (3%/5% below BofAe/consensus; +9% 
QoQ, +28% YoY). We view the QoQ decline in GM/OPM from the record highs in 2Q26

### Page 4

as largely expected, as management had already indicated at the 2Q26 meeting that the 
1H26 GM of 24.7% represented a more sustainable level (see our 2Q26 result note). 
Besides AI, we believe better margins were also helped by improving product mix in 
IT/CE (i.e. higher mix from high-end ICT and LEO satellite) and higher automation level. 
With non-operating gain of NT$1.1bn (including net interest income of NT$369mn and 
FX gain of NT$734mn) and higher minority interest, net income (+4% QoQ, +60% YoY) 
was 12%/11% above BofAe/consensus.

Similar comments on material/power shortage like Delta 
Similar to Delta mgmt.’s comments at 3Q26 analyst meeting, LoT also sees short-term 
shipment uncertainties/push-outs due to material/component shortage (e.g. semis, PCB, 
passives) and power/electricity shortage for AI datacenter infrastructure. Nevertheless, 
LoT’s CEO remains long-term positive on AI demand with ongoing platform/spec 
upgrade. He also expects easing component/power constraints from 4Q26, with ongoing 
strategic component procurement/preparation and customers’ robust AI demand.

4Q26 sales up QoQ; 2H26 GM above 1H; rising AI mix 
Into 4Q26, mgmt. expects cloud sales to grow both QoQ and YoY. Key drivers include (1) 
volume production of 8.5kW PSUs for ASIC applications and BBUs, next-generation 
Nvidia Vera Rubin 110kW solutions, 42-inch power shelves for AMD Helios, (2) prototype 
shipments of 800VDC power rack, and (3) continued momentum in high-end power 
applications, with shipments of power solutions for LEO satellites. the CEO also expects 
2H26 GM to be above 1H26 level. We thus project 4Q26 overall sales to grow 5% QoQ 
(+36% YoY), with GM at 26.3% (vs. 25.9%/24.7% in 3Q26/1H26). For HVDC, the CEO 
further stated 800VDC power rack mass shipment in 1Q27, after final validation and 
sample shipment in 4Q26. Mgmt. kept AI sales mix target at 30%+ in 2026 and 
potentially 40-50% in 2027. LoT raised 2026 capex to NT$19.7bn (vs. NT$18.0bn guided 
at 2Q26 analyst meeting) and expects a higher level in 2027 (BofAe: NT$20.0bn).

Initially expects 2027 sales/GM/OPM YoY increase 
Into 2027, the CEO initially expects cloud sales to grow another 70% YoY, driven by 
rising ASP and power-supply product/spec upgrade. He targets another 10% YoY sales 
growth for optoelectronics business, driven by industrial and new energy business. He 
expects LEO satellite power to grow YoY but sees flattish sales from conventional ICT 
power business. Nevertheless, he expects GM/OPM YoY expansion in 2027. We thus 
expect 2027 sales to grow 38% YoY, with GM/OPM increasing by 1.6ppt/1.5ppt YoY.

### Page 7

Price objective basis & risk 
Delta Electronics Inc. (DLTEF) 
Our PO of NT$2,250 is based on 30x 4Q27-3Q28E EPS, which is close to 2.0 standard 
deviations above Delta's average trading P/E since 2012. We believe the P/E multiple is 
justified, given (1) continuous strength and leading position in server power-supply and 
power choke business, (2) content/value growth with broader product offering in AI 
(artificial intelligence) business, (3) rising cooling sales by targeting top CSPs' 
customized projects, and (4) ongoing margin expansion.

The upside risks to our PO are (1) stronger-than-expected server/datacenter demand, (2) 
faster-than-expected IA recovery, and (3) faster-than-expected EV model launches by 
global brands.

The downside risks to our PO are (1) unexpected changes in policy that could impact IA 
growth in China, (2) slower-than-expected server/datacenter demand recovery, (3) 
earnings impact from unprofitable long-term investments (4) Adverse effects of 
announced USITC 337 investigation into multiple companies in the AI supply chain, 
which includes Delta Electric. 
Lite-On Tech (LOTZF) 
Our PO of NT$288 is based on 18x 2027E EPS, which is around 1.75SD above its 
historical average since 2013. We believe the P/E multiple is justified, given its solid 
market position to capture multi-year AI server power-supply market demand with 
improving product mix. Yet, we also assign a valuation discount to its key peer, given its 
late entry and a slower progress in cooling business, smaller scale, and lighter margin 
profile.

Downside risks: (1) weaker-than-expected demand from server/cloud power-supply 
business, (2) lighter margin profile for automotive LED lighting with limited ASP 
expansion, (3) pricing competition and component price increase from power-supply 
business, and (4) less order win from cooling business.

Upside risks: (1) stronger-than-expected margin profile for automotive LED lighting with 
ASP expansion, (2) faster revenue growth from server/cloud power-supply with a more 
positive datacenter market outlook, and (3) larger/stronger order win from cooling 
business.

### Page 1

ab 5 November 2026
Global Research
First Read
Elan Microelectronics
Q426E likely to be the near-term trough
Q326 earnings below on softer NB demand environment
Elan's Q326 EPS of NT$0.58 (net profit NT$168mn) was below UBSe/street of NT
$1.29/1.71, as a result of lower revenue amid weaker NB demand environment. Q326
revenue was already reported at NT$2.2bn (-31% QoQ), below UBSe/street of NT
$2.4/2.8 bn as NB brand customers further reduced their orders from late-Aug on softer

end-demand after retail price hike and higher component inventories. GM was down
2.7ppts QoQ to 44.7% on less favourable mix, directionally in-line with UBSe of ~2ppt
QoQ decline. It booked non-op loss of NT$100mn, vs NT$294mn gain in Q226, mainly
due to unrealized loss on financial assets. Despite softer earnings, Elan remains
committed on its high dividend yield policy and is considering to raise its payout.
Near-term demand remains muted but Q426E likely to be the trough

Elan is restricted from providing Q4 financial guidance given the accumulated non-op
gain in past four quarters exceeded 10% of the net profit. With October sales reported
at NT$461 mn (-23% MoM), Elan expects Q426 remains a challenging quarter amid
softer demand environment and ongoing inventory adjustment. Nevertheless, we think
channel inventory is on track to be depleted in the coming months and Q426 is likely to
mark the trough for near-term revenue. We forecast Q426E sales to decline 38% QoQ in

Q426E but will rebound by 27% QoQ in Q127E on restocking. Into 2027, Elan noted it
has won some new projects at top NB brands, while also sees share gain opportunities
following its peer's business restructure, as well as ramping low-cost solution to capture
market trend. It also see good progress of haptics (2026 sales 5-6x vs 2025) and single
layer on-cell (3-4x), and we believe the momentum should continue into 2027.
ADAS and Drone remain key long-term growth drivers

Elan's AI-related revenue reached 14% of sales in Q326 and is expected to further
increase in Q426F as AI revenue remains more resilient vs NB-related products. Its ADAS
revenue is primarily driven by EV bus now and it thinks the proliferation into logistic
vehicles should be the next driver. Its drone-related business is on track to see higher
revenue contribution from 2027F as it has penetrated into Thunder Tiger's supply chain,
as well as two other customers, supplying both domestic and overseas demand. Elan

noted on top of the camera modules, its obstacle avoidance system, which carries much
higher ASP of US$800, could start to ramp from Q127F. We forecast its AI-business to
see a 45% sales CAGR in 2025-30E, outpacing traditional HMI.
Valuation: Maintain buy with a NT$150 price target
We cut 2026/27/28E EPS to NT$6.35/6.94/11.55 from NT$8.19/10.82/13.28 on softer
NB demand and prolonged inventory adjustment. We lower our PT to NT$150 (from NT
$170) using a lower multiple of 15x H227-H128E PE (from 16x 2027E P/E) to factor in

overall market valuation contraction. We view current headwinds as cyclical and believe
the market underappreciates Elan's AI opportunities and   upside from share gain.

Company Description
Elan Microelectronics is a Taiwan-based fabless IC design house specialising in human-
machine interface including touchscreen controllers, touchpad modules, pointing stick
modules and fingerprint sensors, specifically for notebook PCs. It started with
microcontrollers (MCUs) for PC peripherals and consumer electronics upon establishment in

1994, and further expanded the reach into notebook PC input devices through the
acquisition of a related subsidiary under KOA Corporation in 2003.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on a target PE multiple.
Fabless integrated circuit (IC) design houses face a variety of uncertainties, including rapidly
changing technology, end-market demand, competition, employee turnover and foundry
wafer costs. They are also vulnerable to legal disputes over intellectual property rights.

For Elan, we believe key risks include: 1) PC and NB demand dynamics, with any slowdown in
these end-markets directly impacting Elan's growth and also its new product roll-outs; 2) a
longer-than-expected product qualification cycle or slower-than-expected product adoption
among clients; 3) fiercer-than-expected price competition from global and Asian peers
negatively impacting growth and gross margin; and 5) faster-than-expected foundry and
assembly cost increases negatively impacting gross margin.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

### Page 1

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  1

2026/10/30 投資速報

電子–半導體 
創意 (3443.TT) 
持續優於預期，CPU 配比提升將成為趨勢

強力買進(維持評等) 
出刊緣由：財報公佈

個股與大盤走勢

近一個月 近三個月 近半年 近一年 
加權指數報酬率 -2.7 15.8 18.0 63.9 
個股股價報酬率 -3.6 131.9 86.3 422.1

營收走勢圖

研究員/吳大任 TERRY.WU@uni-psg.com

研究部對創意的投資建議維持強力買進，係因:(1)3Q26 營收、毛利
率、營業利益率皆優於預期，(2)4Q26 雖因為 ECB 將送件未給財測，
但客戶對 CPU 需求來看仍持續上修 ，(3)北美車用晶片有望在 2Q27 初
導入 ， 中國車用客戶晶片市佔率持續提升 ， 且持續拓展海外客戶 ，27-
28 年貢獻幅度大幅跳升 ，(4)晶片大廠持續上修 CPU 與加速器的配適
性，更有效率的使用 GPU 之效能，(5)不僅 200 系列晶片，CSP 客戶
的 300 系列晶片創意也有所著墨。綜合以上，上修創意 26 年 EPS 預
估為 63.3 元(原估 54.76 元，+125%YOY)，同步上修 27 年 EPS 預估
為 245.9 元(原估 214.5 元，+288.5%YOY)， 及上修28 年EPS 為 467.7
元(原估 392.6 元，+95.9%YOY，以股本膨脹 3%計算)。考量 27 年下
半年創意的成長動能更加明確上修 ， 且費用控管十分優異 ， 客戶從雲
端 AI 延伸至終端 AI，CPU、加速器、自駕晶片及 CPO 皆有著墨，故
對創意看法更加樂觀，故先前 8600 元目前價到價後，仍維持強力買
進投資建議，目標價調升至 9850 元(27 年 EPS*40XPER OR 28 年
EPS*21XPER)。

重點摘要： 
1. 3Q26 營收大幅優於預期，OPM 更加優化：  
創意 3Q26 營收達到創新高且優於上修後的預期的 187.5 億
元，成長35%QOQ，成長117.7%YOY，其中量產營收達到166.7
億元，成長44%QOQ，推估來自於CPU 的貢獻較大，且在2H26
創意也有新增一些產能支援 ， 且NRE 也達到預估的區間上緣 ，
僅衰退 11%QOQ。 毛利率達到20.59%， 優於研究部預期的19%，
更優於市場預估的 19-20%，推估主要是出貨的晶圓良率更加
提升所致。費用控制非常良好，3Q26 費用僅 13.09 億元，低
於 2Q26 的 13.18 億元，但營收卻成長35%QOQ，故使得OPM 提
升至 13.6%，大幅優於先前預估的11%。稅後淨利達到創新高
的 22.73 億元(原估 16.02 億元)，成長 46.1%QOQ，成長
162.1%YOY，EPS 16.96 元(原估 11.96 元)。

2. 因 ECB 將送件，故無 4Q26 財測，但仍正向看待：  
因為創意的 ECB 即將送件，故此次財報後無法給出財測，但
研究部仍認為 4Q26 創意營運動能將上修，主要係因先前在上
半年所提到的投片保有量外，仍有多爭取的 WAFER，從創意
3Q26 每個月的量產營收就可以觀察到持續增加，單月量產營
收已經提升至 65 億元，預估 4Q26 將能持續穩定攀升。另外
NRE 方面，CSP 下一代 CPU 將預估在 4Q26 TAPE OUT，NRE 預

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

估將翻倍成長 。 綜合以上 ，因為量產營收持續優於預期， 故上
修 4Q26 營收為 244.2 億元(原估 236 億元)，成長30.2%QOQ，
成長 97%YOY，稅後淨利上修預估為續創新高的 30.1 億元(原
估 25.35 億元)，成長32.5%QOQ，成長159.6%YOY，EPS 22.46
元(原估 18.92 元)。

3. CPU在Agentic AI世代重要性持續提升： 
根據INTEL的資料中心與人工智慧白皮書顯示，CPU 容量不足
可能造成編排卡頓、工具執行延遲、驗證循環變慢，導致GPU
在等待控制平面 （代理編排） 處理完成時利用率不足 。 若要將
代理型工作負載最佳化，就必須增加 CPU 數量，作為控制與
編排層，在兼顧 新式 AI 工作流程分散且多步驟本質的同
時，將資料高效提供給加速器。故根據INTEL實測顯示，在模
型大小為8B規模時 ， 建議加速器與CPU配比為1.4:1， 模型大小
擴大到70B時，建議配比為1:1。故在AMD簡報也顯示，2025-
2030年CPU產值規模將從260億美元提升至2030年的2200億美
元，CAGR達到50%。創意的CSP客戶所ASIC走到第八代後，CPU
與TPU的配置從先前1:4確實提升至1:2，並且全數從先前的
X86 BASE更改成自家研發的ARM BASE CPU。而CSP客戶第八代
產品放量時間點將落在4Q26末，2027年完整貢獻 ， 故從創意量
產營收看出 ， 因為配比提升CSP客戶對CPU需求持續增加 ， 故上
修CPU26年貢獻創意營收從12億美元上修至13-15億美元。在
27年方面，有鑑於客戶對於加速器及CPU的配飾性相對於第七
代提升一倍，加上下一代CPU將在4Q26 TAPE OUT，有望在2H27
開始貢獻。因為二代產品單價提升，故上修CPU貢獻營收程度
從35億美元上修35-40億美元。 
CSP二代CPU將有望在28年完整貢獻，ASP提升幅度較大，且三
代CPU目前已經開始著手研發 ， 故研究部認為 ，CPU對加速器的
配置開始往1:1走後，創意目前在客戶需求延續性強，且持續
有拿到次世代案件下，28年CPU貢獻幅度將達到50%YOY增長。

圖二、AMD 對 SERVER CPU 產值的預估

資料來源：AMD，統一研究部整理

車用將成為 27 年開始的新成長支柱 
(1)北美客戶： 
根據供應鏈訪查 ， 北美車用客戶晶片量產時間點將落在2Q26，
創意目前在 5 代晶片進度仍優於競爭對手，故以目前規劃的
投片量來看 ， 研究部維持先前預估貢獻約12 億美金營收不變 。
但根據北美客戶法說，該客戶希望將5 代晶片在 2027 年 4 月
導入機器人使用 ， 故研究部目前僅估算車用的部分 ， 尚未估計
機器人，若該客戶順利量產機器人，則貢獻創意營收有望上
修。 
(2)中國客戶： 
以中國客戶自行揭露的資料來看，全系列征程系列晶片出貨
套數已經高達 1000 萬套，在 26 年 9 月，該客戶的 CEO 在社
群媒體上表示，公司智駕晶片出貨量已達 1500 萬套。另外客
戶給出 26 年征程系列晶片將出貨 500 萬套，27 年將更達到
700 萬套的目標。先前預估 27 年以客戶出貨目標 700 萬套的
50%，350 萬套第 7 代征程晶片做估計，貢獻營收將達到 15-
20 億美元，此預估沒有變化，靜待客戶完成年報公告後再行
調整。

圖三、中國自駕車晶片公司 CEO 微博

資料來源：WEIBO，統一研究部整理

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

兩大車用客戶對創意來說 ，27年僅是開端 ， 首先北美客戶27年
才會導入5代晶片，且是機器人及無人計程車皆會導入，附帶
一提，先前展示的飛天跑車也是搭載5代晶片，故認為在客戶
量產機器人及無人計程車後，晶片需求在 28年將會超過翻倍
成長 。 另外在中國客戶方面， 除了本土市場外 ， 該公司積極拓
展海外市場，7代晶片LIFECYCLE有望比6代更久，28年目前也
以翻倍的狀況預估。

4. CSP客戶三代晶片仍有著墨機會： 
北美另一雲端客戶在10月初表示，200系列加速器已經實現量
產，該客戶已經在各地區DATA CENTER 部屬200系列晶片，而
200系列晶片後段為創意打造，推估LIFE CYCLE出貨量為數十
萬顆 ， 貢獻營收約3-5億美元 。 而在300系列方面 ， 先前市場傳
出主要供應商為美國晶片同業 ， 後來研究部訪查 ， 創意在27年
亦有BOOKING 2nm產品，而300系列晶片由N2P節點打造，故推
估創意亦有部分300系列晶片相關生意，故仍有營收貢獻。而
目前推估400系列晶片有望重回創意，挹注 28年後的營運動
能。

5. 26、27年上修預估，28年持續高度成長： 
創意3Q26營收已經大幅優於預期 ， 加上毛利率擊敗市場預期 ，
另外費用控管得宜 ， 使得3Q26 OPM不減反增 ， 另外4Q26雖然未
給財測，但在產能有所支持加上CPU需求持續推升下，營收也
略有上修，故上修創意26年營收預估為685.2億元(原估648.6
億元)，成長100.7%YOY，稅後淨利上修預估為84.8億元(原估
73.28億元)， 成長125%YOY，EPS上修預估為63.3元(原估54.76
元)。 展望27年，CPU貢獻性持續上修 ， 另外也維持車用客戶的
預估值，另外本來就有CSP客戶的200系列加速器開始大幅量
產，300系列目前有所著墨，故上修27年營收預估為2458.5億
元(原估2328億元)，成長258.8%YOY，稅後淨利上修預估為
329.5億元(原估287.5億元)， 成長288.5%YOY，EPS上修預估為
245.9元(原估214.5元)。最後，雖然目前28年投片等尚未確
定 ， 不過創意在CSP二代CPU、 中國及北美車用晶片 ， 接續世代
晶片皆有持續獲得新世代案件 ， 另外在CSP ASIC方面 ， 目前有
將從整合封裝能力開始切入 ，CSP 300/400系列晶片皆有斬獲 ，
故研究 部粗估28年創意營收將上修預估為 5145.8億元(原估
4627.5億元)，成長109.3%YOY，稅後淨利預估上修為645.5億
元(原估542億元)，成長95.9%YOY，EPS預估為467.7元(原估
392.6元，以膨脹後3%股本計算)。

Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-06

### Page 1

软银 2032 年到期美元债单⽇下跌 0.9 美分，亚洲投资级美元债利差升⾄ 2025 年 7 ⽉以来最
宽。不过，信贷市场跌幅仍相对克制，部分机构认为当前更像拥挤交易去化与估值重置，
AI ⻓期算⼒需求尚未动摇。
摘要：
⼈⼯智能股票的抛售浪潮已从股市蔓延⾄信贷市场，软银集团⾸当其冲，旗下美元债创下近⼗
周最⼤单⽇跌幅。
软银 2032 年到期美元债周五下跌 0.9 美分，其股价盘中⼀度重挫 16% 。与此同时，亚洲投资级美
元债利差扩⼤⾄少 3 个基点，交投活跃的亚洲⾼收益债也承压明显。信贷市场的波动叠加财政⾚
字攀升、油价⾛⾼及通胀升温等多重压⼒，令本已因 AI ⽀出热潮⽽获得提振的全球经济⾯临新的
下⾏⻛险。
此番抛售是全球信贷市场更⼤范围重新定价的延续。由 AI 热潮驱动的举债狂潮已推⾼收益率，⽽
本轮股市动荡进⼀步加速了这⼀进程。据彭博汇编数据，亚洲投资级美元债平均利差周四已升
⾄ 73 个基点，为 2025 年 7 ⽉以来最宽。
信贷抛售⼒度仍逊于股市
尽管信贷市场承压，但此轮抛售的烈度明显低于权益市场。 CreditSights 分析师 Zerlina Zeng 指

出，美国国债收益率下⾏为关注总体收益率的投资者提供了⼀定缓冲。
" 美国和亚洲市场的抛售整体较为克制， "Zeng 表⽰。她补充称， 10 ⽉份的⼤幅下跌已吸引不少
投资者逢低买⼊部分亚洲⾼收益债，其中包括表现尚可的房地产信⽤债。

⼼许可证⽅⾯的负⾯消息，均在进⼀步压制利差表现。

Document 344: Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation

Source PDF: all report pdf/Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

Downgrade of global 
equities to a Core allocation  
Summary 
Event: We are downgrading global equities from Overweight 
to a Neutral/Core allocation. We continue to maintain a 
Neutral allocation to fixed income, Overweight on Gold and 
Underweight on Cash. There are more details in our Global 
Market Outlook publication on 30 October 2026. 
What does the equity downgrade mean?  
This downgrade implies a slightly lower allocation to global 
equities within a diversified portfolio. For our CIO Foundation 
Balanced portfolio, this would imply an equity allocation of 
52.5%, down from 53 .2%. We continue to expect positive 
returns for global equities  in the coming 6 -12 months , 
though equity investors may need to recalibrate expectations 
lower, compared with the 15 -20% annualised gains 
experienced in the last few years. Our capital market 
assumptions still suggest a mid -to-high single digit annual 
returns over the next few years, consistent with a transition 
to a mid -cycle bull market . Consensus estimates point to 
16% earnings growth for global equities in 2027, which 
should overcome various headwinds to deliver positive 
returns for equities over the next 12 months. Investors should 
thus, continue to maintain exposure to equities  within a 
diversified portfolio. 
In addition, we see a changing playbook  to prepare for 
higher volatility, more active management and greater 
diversification, as discussed below. 
What does it mean for regional equities 
positioning? 
Within a global equities portfolio, we remain Overweight US 
and Asia ex -Japan (AxJ) equities , which are expected to 
deliver the highest earnings growth in 2027 amongst the 
major regions. This growth is led by the technology sector, 
which carries the largest weight in both regions. Within the 
diversified equities portfolio, we maintain Core allocations to

Allocation to global equities decrease to 52.5% from 
53.2% for our CIO Foundation Balanced portfolio

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 30 October 2026

Euro area equities, which offer lower valuations, and Japan 
equities, which is backed by a reflating economy. We are 
Underweight UK equities, where the sector composition 
offers lower growth prospects in 2027. Within the AxJ region, 
we are Overweight Taiwan equities, with attractive structural 
growth driven by its semiconductor industry. We are also 
Overweight India equities, which offers diversified non-tech 
growth drivers. Taiwan and India equities both generate 
attractive returns on equity. We maintain Core allocations to 
China equities, with low valuations, and Korea equities, with 
explosive earnings growth in its semiconductors industry, but 
volatile price movements. We are Underweight ASEAN, 
which offers relatively lower earnings growth. 
What are the implications for equity sectors? 
We are Overweight these sectors globally: US 
information technology, US healthcare, US financials, US 
materials, Europe ex -UK financials, and Japan 
financials. 
A framework we use is to break the market into four key 
sector building blocks. Tech is the largest block, 
encompassing the Information Technology, Communication 
Services and Consumer Discretionary sectors. Put 
differently, the segment spans five major industries: 
semiconductors, hardware, internet, software, and IT 
services. Within the US market, we expect this segment to 
deliver c.30% earnings growth in 2027, extending the strong 
momentum seen in 2026. 
Financials and Real Estate  are the next building block, 
comprising these two sectors. We expect this segment to 
continue delivering low -teens earnings growth in 2027, 
supported by resilient economic activity and healthy credit 
trends. 
Resources include companies across four sectors: 
Industrials, Materials, Energy, and Utilities . We expect the 
segment to generate 10 -15% earnings growth in 2027, 
although Energy is likely to remain the largest drag on overall 
growth due to tougher comparisons and softer energy price 
assumptions for 2027. 
Healthcare & Others  consists primarily of Healthcare and 
Consumer Staples. We expect mid-teen earnings growth for 
the segment in 2027, with a rebound in healthcare earnings 
representing a potential upside surprise. 
Our Overweight sector exposures are drawn from each of the 
four key blocks above. Unlike the last few years , with 
concentrated gains from Tech providing market leadership, 
we see our downgrade in global equities also reflecting a 
broadening in market leadership towards each of the other 
sector blocks apart from Tech.

US and Asia ex-Japan would continue to lead in 
earnings growth in 2027

### Page 3

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 30 October 2026

As structural themes such as AI transition from the rapid 
buildout phase towards monetisation, diversified exposure 
across sectors and regions should become increasingly 
important. 
What are the implications for equity 
opportunistic ideas? 
We have opportunistic ideas on Global High -Dividend 
equities, US healthcare, Euro area banks , MSCI 
Switzerland and Japan banks. The selection represents a 
more defensive stance  currently, with an average beta of 
below 1 relative to global equities (MSCI AC World), but 
attractive drivers nonetheless, for each of the ideas.  
Global High-Dividend offers higher-income equity allocation 
with historically lower volatility than global equities. US 
healthcare is supported by wider adoption of obesity 
treatments, advance s in oncology a nd innovative medical 
devices, that support earnings growth. Euro area banks  
continue to see steady earnings growth adding to their 
excess capital, which is being returned to shareholders via 
attractive dividend and share buyback yields. Japan banks 
continue to enjoy net margin expansion from BoJ policy 
normalisation, as well as corporate governance 
improvements. MSCI Switzerland is a newly initiated idea for 
investors seeking defensive exposure. H ealthcare and 
consumer staples account s for more than half the index 
weight, providing exposure to relatively resilient demand that 
is more insulated from economic cycles and AI trends. It also 
has a sizeable financial sector supported by steady wealth 
management growth and well-capitalised insurers with 
disciplined underwriting and resilient premium income. 
We see the case for more active management  in a mid -
cycle transition, as higher market dispersion should create 
greater opportunities for active sector and stock selection, 
particularly as leadership within equities evolves. 
What are the implications of higher equity 
market volatility?  
A more uncertain political backdrop and p otentially maturing 
growth rates in certain market segments may contribute to 
more frequent bouts of market volatility. Higher volatility 
presents an opportunity to use structured strategies to gain 
exposure to equities. Such strategies may be used to 
generate income or to gain exposure at a discounted entry 
price. Investors can also consider strategies that are 
structured to return principal or partial principal at maturity. 
What alternative strategies are suited to a 
regime of moderating equity market returns?  
Investors can consider alternative strategies such as hedge  
fund strategies or private markets, for those with the capacity

### Page 4

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 30 October 2026

to accept some illiquidity.  
Equity long/short hedge fund  strategies would retain growth 
participation with less market exposure. Fund managers buy 
companies they expect to outperform and short those they 
expect to underperform, seeking to earn the difference 
between the two , and benefit even as total equity market 
returns are moderating. 
Within private markets , private equity provides  a means to 
higher returns with the effects of an illiquidity premium. 
Meanwhile, i nfrastructure offers  differentiated exposure to 
structural growth opportunities, where c ontracted long-term 
revenues reduce dependence on equity-market sentiment. 
What are the implications on fixed income 
and FX markets? 
Bonds: This year’s surge in bond yields means bonds now 
offer a hedge against any equity market weakness. We 
believe the 3 -7-year part of the US bond yield curve offers 
the best hedge against any equity sell -off caused by AI -
related growth worries. This part of the bond curve has priced 
in most Fed rate hike expectations, which could reverse if 
growth concerns return. Meanwhile, we favour US Treasury 
Inflation-Protected Securities (TIPS) as a hedge against any 
fuel price-led inflation scare.  
Credit: For corporate bonds, adopting a defensive stance in 
a volatile environment typically involves: i) increasing credit 
quality, favouring higher-rated issuers; and ii) moving up the 
capital structure, by reducing exposure to subordinated debt 
and favouring secured debt. From a sector perspective, 
large, diversified banks, utilities, and quasi -sovereigns tend 
to be more defensive, particularly relative to cyclical sectors, 
such as metals and mining, exploration, and technology.         
FX: We favour bearish AUD/JPY as a tactical hedge against 
any equity market correction. Review of 21 past S&P500 
correction sessions (2010 -2025) shows AUD weakened 
against USD in 86% of cases, while JPY strengthened 
against USD in 67%, supporting our bearish AUD/JPY 
outlook during any equity market stress. 
— Steve Brice, Global Chief Investment Officer (CIO)  
— Manpreet Gill, CIO, EMEA 
— Raymond Cheng, CFA, CPA, CIO, North Asia 
— Sundeep Gantori, CFA, CAIA, CIO, Equities

Document 362: First Impressions_ RBNZ Monetary Policy Review October 2026 (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/First Impressions_ RBNZ Monetary Policy Review October 2026 (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 16
Status: ok

### Page 1

#rbnz #newzealand
First Impressions: RBNZ Monetary Policy Review October 2026
By Kelly Eckhold & Westpac New Zealand Economics
12:30 October 28 2026 Share
The RBNZ left the OCR unchanged at 2.75% (in contrast to market expectation for a hike,
including ourselves).
The MPC has stuck to its strategy of pausing to assess the impact of tightening already done. There is
a clear split among the MPC members on where the risks lie although it seems clear enough that

higher interest rates are in the future the data evolves as the Committee expects. Key factors to focus
on are data on inflation expectations, the global economy (and potential downside risks there) and the

### Page 2

path of fuel prices.  We expect a 25 bp increase in the OCR in December to 3% and are reverting to
the OCR profile we had up until mid last week.  We don't think the December hike is a done deal as the
data will inform the next move. We will revert with more detail after the press conference. 
Key takeouts from the minutes below.

Media contact
Kelly Eckhold
Chief economist 
+64 9 348 9382
+64 21 786 758
Key takeouts
Category 2 September 2026 meeting 28 October 2026 meeting
The next policy
decision
Future policy decisions will depend
on the Committee’s judgement of the
balance of risks to medium-term
inflation.
Members discussed that information
arriving over the next six weeks
could meaningfully change the
appropriate policy stance in either
direction.
  Conditional on the central economic
outlook, members judged that the
OCR may need to increase further.
All members agreed that tighter
monetary policy is likely to be
required, but remains conditional on
the economic outlook.
  However, the future OCR path is not
pre-determined.

The medium term
direction for the
OCR
The Committee judges that gradually
removing monetary stimulus is
appropriate to return inflation to the
2 percent target mid-point while
supporting growth and employment.
Further OCR increases likely, but
are conditional on the outlook

### Page 3

Category 2 September 2026 meeting 28 October 2026 meeting
  All members agreed that gradually
removing monetary stimulus is
consistent with achieving the
medium-term inflation target,
conditional on the outlook.
If domestic pricing pressures fail to
moderate sufficiently or broaden
beyond what costs and normal pass-
through can explain, this could
warrant further tightening.
  Conditional on the central economic
outlook, members judged that the
OCR may need to increase further.
On the other hand, subdued pricing
pressures could require holding at
future meetings.
    And if the global economy turns
sharply down, materially weakening
demand and the inflation outlook,
this could alter the desired stance,
including leading to monetary
easing.

The neutral OCR   The Committee acknowledged that
the neutral interest rate has a wide
range of plausible estimates.
    Members noted that the increase in
global long-term yields may partly
signal a higher global neutral
interest rate, due to higher inflation
expectations, war destruction and
demand for capital to support AI
investment.
    Higher global interest rates could
bring forward a potential global
downturn, and the neutral interest
rate could decline rapidly in the
event of an abrupt global
adjustment.

### Page 4

Category 2 September 2026 meeting 28 October 2026 meeting

The strength of
economic activity
After lacklustre growth in the June
quarter, New Zealand's economic
recovery has most likely resumed
but remains uneven.
The New Zealand economy
continues to recover, although
conditions remain uneven.
  The recovery is expected to
strengthen and broaden.
The Committee noted that GDP
growth was more than expected
through the first half of 2026.
  The Committee assessed that
growth was lacklustre in the second
quarter of 2026.
Business survey measures point to
continued expansion in economic
activity.
  The economic recovery is expected
to have resumed in the third quarter,
while remaining uneven across
sectors and regions.
Household consumption has been
weak and the aggregate saving rate
is estimated to be 3.7 percent,
above the 10-year average of 1
percent.

The global
economic
outlook
The global economy is facing
significant risks that could affect
commodity prices and demand for
exports.
Global growth has been resilient but
is increasingly fragile given
geopolitical tensions, high energy
prices, financial stresses, and
surging AI-related investment.
  Despite recent geopolitical events,
economic growth in New Zealand’s
trading partners has remained
resilient and the outlook has
improved.
The Committee assessed that there
are significant risks to the global
economic outlook.

### Page 5

Category 2 September 2026 meeting 28 October 2026 meeting
  Although risks to the global economy
remain heightened, the Committee
expects global economic conditions
to remain favourable for New
Zealand’s export facing businesses
over the medium term.
The Committee noted that a global
downturn could occur abruptly.

The labour
market
Conditions in the labour market
should improve as the recovery
gathers pace.
Employment growth remains
subdued and higher prices will
further reduce real incomes and
purchasing power.
  Employment growth has not been
sufficient to fully absorb new
entrants into the labour market and
unemployment is elevated,
particularly in Auckland and
Wellington and for youth and the
long-term unemployed.
Employment intentions remain
weak, pointing to continued slow
recovery in the labour market.
  The labour market is expected to
slowly improve, with employment
growing and unemployment falling
as the economy recovers.
To date, wage growth has been
subdued, and medium-term inflation
expectations have remained stable.
  On balance, the Committee
assesses that spare capacity
remains in the economy, particularly
in the labour market.

### Page 6

Category 2 September 2026 meeting 28 October 2026 meeting
The short term
inflation outlook
Inflation increased to 4.1 percent in
the June quarter because of higher
fuel prices arising from the conflict in
the Middle East.
Annual inflation is expected to
increase to 4.4 percent in the
December quarter, largely reflecting
current futures prices for fuel.
  The Committee expects inflation to
remain elevated this year before
returning to the target band by mid-
2027 and reaching the 2 percent
mid-point later next year.
Near-term inflation forecasts are
uncertain given volatility in prices for
oil and refined fuels.
    Direct effects of higher fuel prices
will drop out of the annual inflation
calculation in late 2027 if prices for
refined petroleum products stabilise
or fall.

The medium term
inflation outlook
Core inflation, expected wage
growth, and inflation expectations
remain consistent with inflation
returning to the 1 to 3 percent target
band by mid-2027 and the 2 percent
target midpoint later next year.
The Committee is focused on
returning inflation to the 2 percent
target mid-point over the medium
term, discounting temporary
disturbances to inflation and
avoiding unnecessary economic
instability.
  Longer-term inflation expectations
remain near 2 percent, while most
measures of one and two-year-
ahead inflation expectations have
fallen since May.
Impacts on shortterm pricing
behaviour appear to have been
contained and medium-term inflation
expectations remain well anchored.
  Currently, indicators of medium-term
inflation are consistent with inflation
returning to target.
But the longer inflation remains high,
the greater the risk of higher costs
feeding into inflation expectations
and price- and wage-setting
behaviour, making inflation more
persistent.

### Page 7

Category 2 September 2026 meeting 28 October 2026 meeting

The balance of
risks for
economic growth
and inflation
New Zealand’s economic recovery
could be stronger or weaker than
expected and price pressures could
generate more persistent inflation.
New Zealand’s recovery could be
stronger than expected but could
also lose momentum if the global
environment deteriorates or export
strength does not flow through to
jobs, incomes and household
spending.
  Consumption growth could be
subdued if employment and house
price growth remain weak, or if
precautionary behaviour persists.
There is a risk that inflation becomes
entrenched before conventional
expectations measures respond,
despite the previous two OCR
increases.
  Alternatively, consumption growth
could be stronger and absorb spare
capacity faster than currently
anticipated.
In addition, the world economy could
tip abruptly into a downturn causing
weaker inflation over the medium
term.
  All members agreed that downside
risks to activity were significant and
that the recovery could remain
uneven.
It is also possible that geopolitical
escalation could drive fuel prices
higher while weakening global
economic activity at the same time.

Key arguments
advanced by
more hawkish
MPC members
Hayley Gourley, Karen Silk,
Prasanna Gai and Anna Breman
saw upside risks to inflation relative
to the central projection.
Hayley Gourley, Paul Conway and
Karen Silk favoured increasing the
OCR by 25 basis points at this
meeting.

### Page 8

Category 2 September 2026 meeting 28 October 2026 meeting
  These members observed that more
persistence in energy and
petrochemical prices could raise
near-term inflation risks, impact
price-setting behaviour, and lead to
more sticky inflation over the
medium term.
These members emphasised that
core inflation has been above levels
consistent with headline inflation at
the target mid-point for some time.
  Price-setting can be a front-loaded
process, meaning businesses could
raise domestic prices more than
import costs warrant.
This increases the risk of inflation
becoming entrenched in price-
setting behaviour as the conflict and
its consequences endure, including
through higher freight and shipping
costs.
  These factors increase the risk that
monetary policy needs to lean
against broader inflation pressures.
Acting now would guard against
sustained inflation pressures and
potentially reduce the risk of needing
to do more with interest rates later.

Key arguments
advanced by
more dovish MPC
members in each
meeting
Paul Conway and Carl Hansen saw
risks to inflation as balanced.
Anna Breman, Carl Hansen,
Rebecca Williams and Prasanna Gai
favoured holding the OCR at this
meeting as two recent OCR
increases allow the Committee to
take further time to assess their
effects and economic developments.
  These members shared concerns
that high inflation could become
embedded through price-setting
behaviour.
Actual inflation was lower than
expected, reflecting smaller indirect
effects of higher fuels prices, and
medium-term inflation expectations
and wage growth remain consistent
with returning inflation to the target
mid-point.

### Page 9

Category 2 September 2026 meeting 28 October 2026 meeting
But weighed against this, they saw
activity risks as skewed to the
downside and noted that these could
weigh on inflation.
These members emphasised the
risk of a weak recovery, particularly
in household consumption and
investment.
They highlighted the potential effects
of weak house prices and
precautionary behaviour, weakening
growth in household spending.
They favoured pausing at this

meeting to observe and assess
demand and financial conditions.

► 近一週美股 、日股及新興市場表現遜於港股
及歐股 ，因市場擔憂民主黨全面掌控國會恐
影響AI基礎建設擴張進程 ，令股市承壓 ，即
便總統川普表態接受共和黨敗選結果並願尋
求民 主 黨 的跨 黨 合 作，仍 無 法緩 解 市 場擔
憂，美股主要指數普遍收跌；歐股方面 ，面
對全球科技股評價修正 ，部分資金轉入歐洲

市場尋求避險 ，使歐股近期呈現跌深反彈；
亞股方面 ，日股因整體 AI供應鏈與美國高度
連結而同步下跌 ，港股則是受惠騰訊發布新
AI模型帶動相關版塊大漲。

費、醫療保健、公用事業等防禦性產業。

熱門議題
► 黃金價格歷經第二季修整 ，近日呈現大幅反
彈走勢 ，主要係因美國期中選舉後 ，市場憂
心AI產業監管將趨嚴 ，加上晶片與科技股遭
遇大幅修正 ，促使部分資金轉往黃金等防禦
型資產尋求避險。
► 根據世界黃金協會最新報告 ，第3季全球央行
淨買進321公噸黃金，較第二季明顯回升，年
增率達42%，創下自2024年第四季以來的單
季新高，前3季累計買進金額更是高達 675公
噸，顯示黃金儲備已經列入全球央行必需資
產配置而且比重維持穩定增長態勢。
► 長期以來黃金具有抗通膨特性 ，觀察黃金與
聯邦基金利率走勢 ，從2020年之後 ，即便全
球歷經2次升息循環，黃金價格仍呈現穩健上
揚走勢 ，反應其抗通膨保值特性 。此外，美
國財政赤字壓力難消 ，恐令美元長期購買力
被稀釋 ， 加 速 全 球 央 行 去 美 元 化， 有 鑑 於
此，建議黃金納入資產配置。

► 醫療類股於美國第三季財報展現強勁的獲利
韌性，實際獲利優於預期程度為主要產業中
表現最佳，背後主要受惠大型製藥企業 (如：
禮來、再生元製藥、吉利德科學)在減重藥、
癌症治療及創新藥領域之需求持續增長 ，帶
動整體產業獲利表現優於市場預期。
► 期中選舉後醫療政策輪廓逐漸明朗 。民主黨
主張支持擴大醫療保險覆蓋 、延續平價醫療
法案補貼以及緩和醫療補助計劃 (Medicaid)
削減，有助提升醫療服務使用率；雖藥價談
判政策恐帶來部分定價壓力 ，但整體政策主
張仍對創新藥 、生技研發及醫療保險產業具
潛在政策支持效果。
► 綜上，在AI投資回報率面臨市場檢驗之際 ，
市場資金持續尋求防禦性配置 。醫療保健類
股與資訊科技類股相關性偏低 ，有助分散投
資組合對單一 AI題材依賴 ，加上第三季獲利
動能轉強 、創新藥需求穩健及產業能見度提
升，後續有望持續受惠資金輪動效應。

財報與政策題材雙利多，醫療類股成市場新寵兒

政策議題 民主黨傾向 共和黨傾向 兩黨共識對製藥產業潛在影響
降低藥價 依《通膨削減法案》框架，擴大醫療補助計劃(Medicaid)藥價談判適用範圍 傾向透過最惠國定價機制來降低藥價 兩黨均主張降低藥價製藥企業面臨定價壓力
美國本土製造 透過研發補助、稅收優惠及產業政策鼓勵藥廠回流美國生產 透過關稅、供應鏈重組及企業承諾推動製藥產能回流 兩黨均支持醫藥供應鏈自主化有利本土藥廠及原料藥供應鏈投資
醫療保險覆蓋
支持延長《平價醫療法案》補貼、擴
大醫療補助計劃(Medicaid)覆蓋範圍，
並緩和該補助計劃的削減
傾向由州政府及市場機制主導醫療
體系，限制聯邦政府支出
醫療的可負擔性為兩黨政策共同焦點，有助
增加就醫人口及處方藥使用量，支撐製藥、
醫療服務及保險業長期需求
FDA審查資源 支持增加FDA審查預算與人力配置 著重監管效率優化，額外資源投入相對有限 兩黨均支持提升新藥審查效率利多創新藥與生技公司

投資焦點必需消費
► 短期而言 ，檢視各類股在最近股災表現 ，美
國成長股 、資訊科技 、與通訊服務等高波動
度類股 ，表現明顯落後大盤 ，其中，資訊科
技與通訊服務波動度最大 、跌幅最重 ，主要
係因美股科技巨頭財報不如預期 、負自由現
金流量 ，疊加期中選舉後民主黨恐緊縮 AI基
礎建設進程及資本支出見頂等不確定因素 ，
令市場 擔憂 導致 資 金流出 ；並轉 進核 心消
費、健康護理 、防禦股 、公用事業等防禦性
產業，報酬相對較穩健，受市場資金青睞。
► 長期而言，從2008迄今，核心消費類股長期
表現穩健上漲 。與主要指數相比 ，面對近月
AI科技股修正 ，同期間費半大跌 18%、那斯
達克跌幅 6%、標普跌幅 1%，而核心消費類
股大漲13%，反應其低波動、抗震韌性。
► 整體而言 ，從2015年至今 ，歷經2次通膨與
升息循環 ，核心消費受影響性低 ，有鑑於資
產配置角度 ，建議短期跟隨資金流向配置核
心消費股，提高投組抗震韌性。
必需消費長期表現穩健、抗通膨、低波動避險資金流入，必需消費類股單月表現最佳

配置必需消費類股，提高投組抗震韌性

股市
 隨著美國期中選舉結束 ，美伊衝突可望降溫 ，市場關注焦點將回歸基本面 ，惟AI資料中心建設進程
或將受到州政府緊縮政策而造成評價面壓力增添股價波動 。短期可布局防禦股 、分散配置於不同國
家與產業，增強抗震力、降低市場波動影響 ，長期而言，AI從全面看好變成要追求財務健全度較高
的品質股、且大優於小，震盪之後長期正向趨勢不變。
 歐、日股可分散配置 ，利率走升有利銀行股 。此外，隨財政政策支持 ，歐洲國防軍工仍有投資題材 。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，AI從
全面看好變成要追求財務健全度較高的品
質股、且大優於小，震盪之後長期正向趨
勢不變，非AI部分可關注必需消費產業、
醫療保健產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲國防
債市
 隨資金湧入 ，公債殖利率回落 ，惟根據最新聯準會利率點陣圖顯示 ，官員預估今年底前仍有一碼升
息空間，立場偏向維持較高利率水平抑制通膨發展 。建議以中短天期債券投資為主 ，可逢利率走升
時布局進行鎖利領息 。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流與還款能力 ，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引

力者，包含金融、通訊及公用事業等產業。
債種：中短天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金
融、通訊及公用事業等
匯市
 聯準會12月份會議升息態度偏鷹，美元高檔整理。
 日本銀行9月升息，但前瞻指引偏中性，未如市場預期偏鷹，日圓弱勢盤整。
美元高檔整理，日圓弱勢盤整
商品
 美國期中選舉結束，預期中東地緣衝突降溫，油價從近期高點回落，短線仍偏震盪。
 去美元化支撐全球央行買盤，提供黃金中長期穩定支撐。
低接買盤流入，黃金低點反彈，中長期維
持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產
策略思維
策略思維 短期跟隨資金流向避險，中期擁抱AI政治紅利

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

United States: Elevated inflation and high growth
02/10/2026 4
August 2026: Marked improvement in services
business confidence, from an already high level
September 2026: Manufacturing business

confidence remains stable at a high level
August 2026: Increasing inflationary pressures September 2026: Supplier deliveries do not get
longer
August 2026: Consumers are a bit more
concerned about their personal finances
August 2026: Headline inflation resists to the
upside
U.S. macroeconomic performance has remained solid since the onset of the shock, as illustrated in particular by Q2 growth
of +3.8% and +9% (annualized quarterly rate) in consumer spending and private nonresidential investment, respectively.

In the manufacturing sector, business sentiment has gained momentum despite the shock, while the impact of this shock
on the services sector has remained very limited. The immediate reaction of consumer confidence was, however, more
negative before beginning to improve, which was nevertheless interrupted in August (latest available data for households’
sentiment on their future personal financial situation). The University of Michigan’s composite indicator continued to

deteriorate in September, due to the sharp rise in fuel prices. But declining confidence still does not translate into reduced
spending—at least not at the aggregate level.
On the inflation front, the spike immediately following the outbreak of the conflict in Iran was more pronounced than in
the months following Russia’s invasion of Ukraine. The decline in June still left inflation at a high level, but its stability in
July and August was notable (3.4% y/y), as was the latest—the third consecutive—slight decline in inflation excluding

energy in August (2.5% y/y, same rate as in February). Given the existing inflationary pressures, however, inflation is highly
likely to resume its upward trend in September.
Source: BLS, ISM, NFIB, University of Michigan, BNP Paribas
